Prior activity: 2026-07-31 — Completed quick task 260731-r81: fix "Server connection lost, recovering…" toast that fired when the pretty-view speak button was used on a long assistant message. Ashley bounty-driven (`speak-button-long-message-triggers-connection-lost-popup` — pinned, verbatim parked earlier this session via `/bounty`: "'Server connection lost, recovering…' popup happens when i try to use speak button on a long message"). Root cause diagnosed: both the frontend axios call in `postSpeak` (bound to `authApi`'s default 30s timeout at `main-axios.ts:306`) and the backend `handleSpeak` AbortController (`src/backend/database/routes/voice.ts:147`, `setTimeout(..., 30_000)`) timed out before Chatterbox TTS finished synthesizing long text — one path throws `code=ECONNABORTED` "timeout of 30000ms exceeded" (client trips first), the other returns 504 `{error: "TTS timeout"}` (backend trips first). Either path lands in `main-axios.ts:530`'s response interceptor which calls `dbHealthMonitor.reportDatabaseError()`; the `isBackendUnreachable` predicate matches BOTH `errorCode === "ECONNABORTED"` and `lowerMessage.includes("timeout")`, so it emits `database-connection-degraded` → `AppShell.tsx:646` renders toast `common.connectionDegraded` = "Server connection lost, recovering…" (`locales/en.json:115`), which sticks until any other request succeeds. The 30s cap was inherited unthinkingly from `handleTranscribe`'s T-16-02 STT timeout when patch #223 added TTS support last session — STT clips are small + fast, TTS synthesis scales with text length, and `SPEAK_TEXT_MAX = 25000` chars can take minutes at Chatterbox's rate. Vehicle sizing per ROADMAP check: no `speak-button-connection-lost` phase in ROADMAP.md, ~10 lines of code across two files with no design decisions → `/gsd:quick` (not plan-phase, not ad-hoc — the two-file cross-tree scope + typecheck + full-suite value justify the ceremony). Fix (two atomic code commits on `feat/tab-title-from-tmux`): `d7ab821` bumps `handleSpeak`'s AbortController from `30_000` → `300_000` ms (5 min) with an inline comment explaining the 10× asymmetry vs `handleTranscribe`; `c8f75ae` adds explicit `timeout: 300_000` to `postSpeak`'s axios config object overriding `authApi`'s 30s default, with a matching inline comment. Deliberately NOT touched (per plan leave-alone list, verified via `git diff --name-only HEAD~2 HEAD`): `handleTranscribe` (still 30s — STT scope preserved), `handleListVoices` (still 30s), `getVoices` (still uses authApi 30s default), `main-axios.ts` global default, `db-health-monitor.ts::isBackendUnreachable` predicate, all locale files, all AppShell toast wiring, `ChatMessage.speak.test.tsx` + `IdentityModal.voice.test.tsx` (both mock `postSpeak` at the module boundary so the timeout value isn't observed — remained green untouched). Sanity greps corroborate: `setTimeout(..., 30_000)` count in `voice.ts` = 2 (handleTranscribe + handleListVoices only), `setTimeout(..., 300_000)` count = 1 (handleSpeak), `timeout: 300_000` in `voice-api.ts` = 1 (postSpeak only), zero timeout key added to `getVoices`. Verification: `npm run build:backend` EXIT 0 per patch #154 mandatory-for-backend-touching rule; `npm run build` EXIT 0 (frontend bundle 4.28s); full `npx vitest run --reporter=verbose` = **947 passed / 6 skipped / 0 failed across 82 files** — matches the `8e01c93` baseline exactly (no regressions); independent grep of `/tmp/r81-vitest.log` for `FAIL|failed|✗` per tina.md L508 preference (#209→#211 arc) — 17 hits all benign (application-level `[INFO]` log lines from tests that intentionally exercise failure paths + `--reporter=verbose` printing test names containing the word "failed" + expected `Network Error` lines from voice-api error-handling suites); zero `Unhandled|Rejection` matches outside expected test scopes. Two atomic commits scope-clean: `git show --name-only d7ab821` = 1 file (`src/backend/database/routes/voice.ts`), `git show --name-only c8f75ae` = 1 file (`src/ui/api/voice-api.ts`) — no identity-side files touched (per h8f-vs-hn8 executor-scope split, Tina updates skynet-patches.md + bounty archive AS ORCHESTRATOR after this row); no git worktrees used (fleet rule 2026-07-31 verified via `workflow.use_worktrees=false` in gsd config); no push, no `docker build`, no `docker compose up --force-recreate` — stopped at commit boundary per fleet rule (Ashley 2026-07-27). Ships as patch #231 in `~/.claude/identities/tina/skynet-patches.md`. Closes pinned bounty `~/.claude/identities/tina/bounties/speak-button-long-message-triggers-connection-lost-popup/` — status stays `in_progress` + `pinned` per through-line rule until it archives on batch deploy (observable fix requires the container to carry the new bundle; #231's fix only takes effect post-recreate). Ninth atomic entry in the pinned-bounties multi-session work-through-all-pinned-bounties arc (after #215 copy-button + #216 overlay-behind-badge + #217 attach-inside-textarea + #218 alphabetical-ordering + #219 identity title+avatar + #220 wakeup-form-editor + #221 message-queue + #222 idle-icon-swap + #223 speak-messages + #224–#228 identity-modal-full-editability Phase 18 + #229 bubble-padding + #230 A+B URL-hash-restore). New `src/ui/features/pretty-view/CopyableBlock.tsx` (~122 lines) wraps `<pre>` / `<blockquote>` renderers inside ChatMessage's ReactMarkdown component overrides with a hover-visible copy button (touch: always-visible via `[@media(pointer:coarse)]:opacity-100`); `navigator.clipboard.writeText` on click with `window.electronClipboard` fallback matching the three existing callsites (deliberately did NOT reach for `RobustClipboardProvider` which is xterm-specific); "Copy" ↔ "Copied" label swap reverting after 1500ms via cleanup-refed setTimeout so unmount-during-copied doesn't leak. Executor auto-fixes: (1) `textContent` extraction clones wrapper + removes button before reading so "Copy"/"Copied" label doesn't bleed into clipboard payload; (2) vitest `navigator` Proxy pattern instead of `Object.defineProperty` because in jsdom the component module sees the real `Clipboard [EventTarget]` through a different reference chain than the test module. Tests: 8 new CopyableBlock behavior tests (A-F: render, both clipboard paths, 1500ms revert, textContent correctness, swallowed-rejection safety) + 3 new ChatMessage regression-guards (G-I: `<pre>` wrapped, `<blockquote>` wrapped, injected-turn/quick-reply/ThumbsUp short-circuits untouched). Last activity (prior): 2026-07-30 — Completed quick task 260730-sjf: fixed the session-recycling overlay firing spuriously on host-reachability trouble. Ashley bounty-driven (`recycling-overlay-fires-when-host-unreachable` — parked this session via `/bounty`, greenlit as next after diagnosis). Two categorical fixes shipped in one arc, motivated by root cause that the Layer-2 discovery repoll's `.then()` callback in `claude-session-server.ts` conflated "we couldn't reach the host to ask" with "we asked and Claude isn't there" — both funneled into `transitionToHolding("discovery_diff")`. **Fix A (commit `4d3ef07`) — narrow the arm:** `queryPanePid` (`src/backend/ssh/tmux-helper.ts`) split its single catch into a two-case contract — rethrow on `execCommand` SSH failure so callers can classify, return null only on unparseable/empty pane_pid output. `discoverClaudeSession` (`src/backend/claude-session/session-file-discovery.ts`) wraps `queryPanePid` in try/catch and returns `{status:"inactive", reason:"exec_error"}` on throw (matching the three existing SSH-throw catches at the walk/PID-file/JSONL-test sites). `claude-session-server.ts` repoll callback hoists `isExecErrorTick` flag: `exec_error` inactive branch is now silent-tick (no `transitionToHolding`), and the `holdingTicks++` block is guarded on `!isExecErrorTick` so SSH failures don't burn the 5-min holding budget. Two tests added to `session-file-discovery.test.ts` (Cases 13+14: queryPanePid rethrow classified as exec_error; unparseable-pid null still classified as no_tmux_session). **Fix B (commit `90e1d85`) — self-clear on same-file recovery:** new `SessionHoldingClearedEvent` variant `{type:"session_holding_cleared"}` added to `ClaudeSessionServerEvent` union in `src/ui/api/claude-session-api.ts`. New backend helper `transitionFromHoldingToActiveSameFile()` (modeled on `transitionToHolding` with matching idempotency guard) flips `changeoverState="active"`, resets `holdingTicks=0`, emits the new WS frame, logs `claude_session_holding_cleared`. Call site: repoll active-branch's same-file path — `if (changeoverState === "holding" && result.sessionFile === currentSessionFile)` — invokes the new helper. Real SIGTERM-fallback recycles unaffected (they take the different-file branch first). Frontend: new `case "session_holding_cleared"` in `PrettyView.tsx`'s onmessage switch calls `setIsHolding(false) + setHoldingTimeoutError(false)` ONLY — deliberately does NOT wipe messages / contextPct / harnessTasks / backgroundedAgents / plan_pending / asideText (the point is the session did NOT change; message stream survives verbatim). Distinct from case `session_changed` which is heavy-reset. Executor auto-fix: rather than mock full WS server + SSH pair (7+ dep mocks to reach `connectToPane`), exported a module-scope `__applyRepollResultForTests` pure test seam mirroring the existing `__handleIdentityCountBountiesForTests` pattern — takes state + helpers as injectable parameters. New file `src/backend/claude-session/claude-session-server.repoll.test.ts` (9 tests): 5 branch cases from spec (a-e) plus 4 sub-cases. 3 new tests in `PrettyView.test.tsx` (F1: overlay unmounts on session_holding_cleared; F2: messages preserved; F3: contextPct preserved via aria-valuenow — auto-fix from initial textContent assertion because contextPct renders through aria-valuenow, not visible text). Verification: `npm run build:backend` EXIT 0 (zero errors — critical because tsc --noEmit alone doesn't catch backend TS errors per patch #154 rule); `npm run build` EXIT 0 (4.39s); full `npm test` = 76 files / 861 passed / 6 skipped / 0 failed. Log grep-gate for `FAIL|failed|✗` returned 0 real failures (verified per the just-promoted L508 learned preference from #211 arc — don't trust summary lines). Note: prior 5 unrelated failures in ComposeBox.voice.test.tsx (from patch #209's jsdom .catch bug) already resolved by patch #211's `9213a5b` (this branch's tip). Last activity (prior): 2026-07-30 — Completed quick task 260730-ptd: wired 4 Google Material Design audio-feedback sounds (start/stop/cancel/error) into the `useVoiceRecording` hook at `src/ui/features/pretty-view/useVoiceRecording.ts`. Task 1 (`be3c573`, assets): copied 4 unmodified Material Design MP3s (state-change_confirm-down → start.mp3; state-change_confirm-up → stop.mp3; navigation-cancel → cancel.mp3; alert_error-02 → error.mp3) into new `src/ui/assets/sounds/mic/` directory + wrote `CREDITS.md` with CC-BY-SA 4.0 attribution + source URL + license URL + verbatim filename mapping. Task 2 (`5c59743`, wire-in + tests): 4 Vite `?url` imports at hook top; 4 `useRef<HTMLAudioElement | null>(null)` slots with inline lazy-init guards (hook mount = only construction site, survives re-renders); `playSound(audio)` helper centralizes `currentTime = 0` reset + `.play().catch(() => {})` silent-failure — audio failure is UX polish and MUST NOT disrupt recording. Placements: start.mp3 in the `start()` `.then()` callback AFTER `recorder.start()` + `setState("recording")` (success path only — permission-denied never plays, per plan spec); stop.mp3 as the very FIRST statement in `endAppend()` + `endSend()` after their state guards (BEFORE any `await`, so user hears feedback before STT network latency); cancel.mp3 as first statement in `cancel()` after guard (BEFORE `stopRecording()` teardown); error.mp3 in all 3 failure branches of `transcribeBlob()` (fetch throw, !res.ok, invalid JSON) + the empty-transcript guard in endAppend/endSend. Test suite `useVoiceRecording.test.ts` grew 8→14 tests: Audio constructor mocked as function-declaration (NOT `vi.fn().mockImplementation(arrow)` — vitest requires real constructor shape for `new` calls); each new test uses `invocationCallOrder` to prove ordering (start.mp3 AFTER recorder.start(), stop.mp3 BEFORE fetch, cancel.mp3 BEFORE recorder.stop()); regression tests cover getUserMedia rejection (start.mp3 NOT played), HTTP 500 + network error (error.mp3 plays), and rejected-play() promise (silently swallowed, state still transitions). No iOS silent-switch code — HTML5 `<audio>` on iOS Safari uses the ambient audio session which honors the silent switch by default (zero code needed). No mute toggle, no transcript-ready chime, no UI-visible attribution surface — all deferred per plan scope. Verification: full `npx vitest run` = 74 files / 841 passed + 6 skipped / 0 failed (was 835 before — 6 new tests); `npx tsc --noEmit` EXIT 0; grep gates pass. Renders outbound fleet Matrix relay PUTs as right-aligned blue bubbles + inbound task-notification receives as left-aligned orange bubbles, so fleet coordination shows up in-flow instead of hiding inside opaque tool-use blobs. OUTBOUND detector: `curl` + `-X PUT` + URL shape `rooms/X/send/m.room.message/Y` (three-way conjunction, kills false positives from prose/comments). INBOUND detector: `type=user` + `origin.kind=task-notification` + recv.sh line regex — non-relay wakes correctly render as neutral. Bubble sender mxid resolved to local identity where possible so hue chain matches. Long-inbound file-pointer support fetches recv.sh's pointer file and renders full body inline. | 260801-54e | Add `composeIntentTransform` layer to pretty-view ComposeBox send pipeline — natural-language doubled-word-at-front pattern (`bounty bounty ...` → `/bounty ...`) that acts as a "spoken slash" for Ashley's voice-first workflow. Ashley design-driven (multi-round design conversation this session, no pre-existing bounty). Design origin: typing `/` is Ashley's one remaining keyboard-reach in an otherwise voice-native workflow; the doubled word is a redundancy STT cannot lose, no natural English sentence starts with a word repeated back-to-back at the front, so false-positive rate is effectively zero. Registry-gated (ships with a single entry `bounty` → `/bounty`) so common English doublings ("no no", "bye bye", "very very") don't fire even if the regex matches. Regex `/^\s*([a-z]+)[\s.,;:!?\-]+\1[\s.,;:!?\-]+(\S.*)$/is`: `[a-z]+` letters-only for keyword precision, `i` flag case-insensitive on both first-word and backref, `s` flag makes `.` cross newlines, punctuation-tolerant on BOTH sides of the second word (faster-whisper habitually inserts commas/periods there — empirically validated 2026-08-01 via scratch/UAT with Ashley's voice), requires at least one whitespace-or-punct char between the doubled words (so `bountybounty` glued form does NOT match) and requires non-empty content after (so bare "bounty bounty" does NOT match — probably an accidental send). Vehicle sizing per ROADMAP check: no phase in ROADMAP.md, ~55-line pure-function module + ~180-line 25-case vitest suite + 4 tiny inline wraps in ComposeBox = one cohesive unit of work with locked design (multi-round scratch/UAT iteration already completed) → `/gsd:quick` (not plan-phase). Scratch path per patch #221 lesson: built in working tree WITHOUT committing → `docker cp dist/assets/. skynet:/app/html/assets/` + `docker cp dist/index.html skynet:/app/html/index.html` → Ashley UAT with real voice → she confirmed "worked!" → `git checkout --` scratch → redo via `/gsd:quick` for the clean shipping commits. Three atomic TDD commits on `feat/tab-title-from-tmux`: `58bd386` (test, RED — 25-case suite: 10 passthrough cases including glued `bountybounty`, mid-sentence doubled `foo bounty bounty add`, `bounty bounty` alone with no content, common English `no no do not do that`, already-slash-prefixed `/bounty add a thing`; 15 transform cases including all 4 Whisper-shape UAT captures verbatim: "Bounty, bounty. Buzz, buzz, I'm a bee." → "/bounty Buzz, buzz, I'm a bee.", "Bounty bounty, bananas are delicious." → "/bounty bananas are delicious.", "bounty bounty. add a thing", "bounty bounty, add a thing"), `0455e76` (feat, GREEN — module implementation with JSDoc header explaining voice-first origin + front-only anchoring + registry gate + requires-content contract + punctuation-tolerance-both-sides + empty-match-returns-passthrough), `85ff865` (feat, integration — wraps exactly 4 `onSend(payload)` call sites in ComposeBox.tsx with `applyIntentTransform(collapseNewlinesForSend(...)).transformed`: `fireQueuedDispatch` L763, `handleQueueSlotSend` L903, `handleSend` primary path L959, `handleVoiceSend` queue-slot branch L1039; plus `import { applyIntentTransform } from "./composeIntentTransform";` at L15 beside other pretty-view sibling imports). Deliberately NOT wrapped (grep-verified): attachment-caption path (`onSendWithAttachments` around L947 — attachments have their own flow, `/bounty` skill can't consume an attachment), reset-send path (`handleResetSend` around L1122 — wraps with `/id reset (...)` prefix, no user-authored front for intent transform to fire on), quick-reply path (`handleQuickSend` around L1153 — sends canned strings like "thumbs up", no user-authored input). Verification: `npx tsc --noEmit` EXIT 0 (clean); scoped `npx vitest run src/ui/features/pretty-view/composeIntentTransform.test.ts` = 25/25 passed; regression `npx vitest run src/ui/features/pretty-view/ComposeBox` = 78/78 passed (exact baseline from scratch iteration preserved); capture-and-grep gate on both vitest logs for `FAIL|failed|✗|Unhandled` per tina.md L508 preference (#209→#211 arc — runner's "0 failed" claim isn't authoritative if unhandled rejections tank tests silently) = 0 real failure markers. | 260801-29v | Fix pretty-view file-upload race — chunk arriving before `activeBatches.set()` emitted `upload_failed reason=unknown_temp_id message="no active batch"`. Ashley bounty-driven (`file-upload-unknown-temp-id-no-active-batch` — pinned, verbatim parked earlier session via `/bounty`: "figure out file upload issues (unknown_temp_id: no active batc..)"). Root cause: `src/backend/ssh/terminal.ts:885` dispatched `void handleUploadStart(...)` fire-and-forget. `handleUploadStart` is async and performs real SFTP I/O over the SSH tunnel (`sftp.realpath(".")` + `ensureLandingDir` stat/mkdir + per-file `resolveNonCollidingFinal` stat calls + `createWriteStream`) before it calls `activeBatches.set(mqid, batch)` at pretty-view-upload.ts:460. Client-side pump (`src/ui/features/pretty-view/use-pretty-view-uploads.ts:414`) fires `void pumpBatch(batchId)` immediately after the `upload_start` WS send, so 64KB chunks arrive back-to-back on the same tick. Synchronous `case "upload_chunk"` (terminal.ts:892) then runs `handleUploadChunk` which does `activeBatches.get(mqid)` → undefined → emits the failure at pretty-view-upload.ts:558. Intermittent — hits on cold SFTP session (first upload) or slower I/O; misses on warm sessions. Ashley reproduced with a 162.7 KB text file. Vehicle sizing per ROADMAP check: no phase in ROADMAP.md, one focused unit of work with locked design → `/gsd:quick` (not plan-phase). Fix: added per-WS `pendingStarts: Map<string, Promise<void>>` in terminal.ts's WS closure adjacent to `ownedUploadBatches`; `case "upload_start"` captures the `handleUploadStart` promise, stores it in `pendingStarts` keyed by `messageQueueItemId`, self-cleans with `.finally(() => pendingStarts.delete(mqid))` (still non-blocking at dispatch level — other WS traffic like input/resize is not gated on SFTP setup); `case "upload_chunk"` looks up `pendingStarts.get(mqid)` and if a promise is present chains `handleUploadChunk` via `.then(...)` (deferring the chunk until the batch is registered), else calls `handleUploadChunk` synchronously as today (warm-session fast path preserved verbatim — no spurious microtask on the common case). JS microtask FIFO ordering preserves chunk-arrival order across multiple deferred chunks. No client-side changes, no new WS event, no added round-trip. Two atomic commits on `feat/tab-title-from-tmux`: `49cba54` (fix — +48/-9 in terminal.ts) then `d46e1bd` (regression test — +173 in pretty-view-upload.test.ts, two new `describe` blocks reusing existing mocks). Test A (race): fires `upload_start` and `upload_chunk` on the same tick with SFTP realpath/mkdir mocks delayed via `setTimeout(r, 0)`, asserts no `upload_failed reason=unknown_temp_id message="no active batch"` emitted AND chunk lands (`upload_progress` fires). Test B (fast path): after start settles, chunk runs synchronously with no `.then()` deferral. Race-capture proof: executor temporarily reverted the test-local dispatcher to the pre-fix fire-and-forget shape → Test A failed with exactly the expected error string → restored fixed helpers → Test A passes; confirms the test is sensitive to the race the terminal.ts change fixes. Verification: `npm run build:backend` EXIT 0 per tina.md L508 mandatory-for-backend-touching rule; `npm run build` EXIT 0 (4.69s); `npx vitest run src/backend/ssh/pretty-view-upload.test.ts` = 17/17 passed. Scope check per commit: `git show --name-only 49cba54` = 1 file (`src/backend/ssh/terminal.ts`), `git show --name-only d46e1bd` = 1 file (`src/backend/ssh/pretty-view-upload.test.ts`) — no changes to `pretty-view-upload.ts` orchestrator, no client-side edits, no identity-side files touched (per h8f-vs-hn8 executor-scope split — Tina updates skynet-patches.md + bounty archive AS ORCHESTRATOR after this row); no git worktrees used (fleet rule 2026-07-31 verified via `workflow.use_worktrees=false`); no push, no `docker build`, no `docker compose up`, no deploy at commit time. Ships as patch #239 in `~/.claude/identities/tina/skynet-patches.md`. Closes pinned bounty `~/.claude/identities/tina/bounties/file-upload-unknown-temp-id-no-active-batch/`. Eleventh atomic entry in the pinned-bounties multi-session work-through-all-pinned-bounties arc. | 260731-r81 | Fix "Server connection lost, recovering…" toast that fired when the pretty-view speak button was used on a long assistant message. Ashley bounty-driven (`speak-button-long-message-triggers-connection-lost-popup` — pinned, verbatim parked earlier session via `/bounty`). Root cause: both the frontend axios `postSpeak` (bound to `authApi`'s default 30s at `main-axios.ts:306`) and the backend `handleSpeak` AbortController (`voice.ts:147`, `setTimeout(..., 30_000)`) timed out before Chatterbox TTS finished synthesizing long text — one path throws `ECONNABORTED`, the other returns 504 `{error: "TTS timeout"}`; both land in `main-axios.ts:530`'s response interceptor which calls `dbHealthMonitor.reportDatabaseError()`, `isBackendUnreachable` matches on `ECONNABORTED` and on the `"timeout"` substring rule, and the toast fires. 30s cap was inherited unthinkingly from `handleTranscribe`'s T-16-02 STT timeout when patch #223 added TTS last session — STT clips are small + fast, TTS synthesis scales with text length, and `SPEAK_TEXT_MAX = 25000` can take minutes at Chatterbox's rate. Vehicle sizing: no phase in ROADMAP.md, ~10 lines across two files, no design decisions → `/gsd:quick` (not plan-phase). Two atomic code commits on `feat/tab-title-from-tmux`: `d7ab821` bumps `handleSpeak`'s AbortController to `300_000` ms (5 min) with an inline comment explaining the 10× asymmetry vs `handleTranscribe`; `c8f75ae` adds explicit `timeout: 300_000` to `postSpeak`'s axios config object overriding the 30s default, with matching inline comment. Deliberately NOT touched (verified via `git diff --name-only HEAD~2 HEAD`): `handleTranscribe` (still 30s), `handleListVoices` (still 30s), `getVoices` (still authApi 30s default), `main-axios.ts` global default, `db-health-monitor.ts::isBackendUnreachable` predicate, all locale + AppShell toast wiring, `ChatMessage.speak.test.tsx` + `IdentityModal.voice.test.tsx` (both mock `postSpeak` at the module boundary so timeout value isn't observed — remained green untouched). Sanity greps corroborate: `30_000` count in `voice.ts` = 2, `300_000` count = 1, `timeout: 300_000` in `voice-api.ts` = 1, zero timeout key in `getVoices`. Verification: `npm run build:backend` EXIT 0 per patch #154 mandatory-for-backend-touching rule; `npm run build` EXIT 0 (4.28s); full `npx vitest run --reporter=verbose` = **947 passed / 6 skipped / 0 failed across 82 files** — matches `8e01c93` baseline exactly; independent grep of `/tmp/r81-vitest.log` for `FAIL|failed|✗` per tina.md L508 preference (#209→#211 arc) — 17 hits all benign (application-level failure-path log lines + verbose reporter printing test names containing "failed" + expected voice-api Network Error tests); zero `Unhandled|Rejection` matches outside expected scopes. | 260731-2pa | Replace the raw JSON schedule textarea in `WakeupsTab.tsx` (patch #154's raw editor) with a form-based schedule editor. Ashley bounty-driven (`identity-modal-wakeup-form-editor` — pinned, verbatim: "a proper form UI for editing wakeup schedules inside the identity modal — not a text-edit-the-JSON situation"). Sixth atomic entry in the pinned-bounties multi-session work-through-all-pinned-bounties arc (after #215 copy-button + #216 overlay-behind-badge + #217 attach-inside-textarea + #218 alphabetical-row-ordering + #219 identity title+avatar). Design work-through: I threw together an interactive HTML mockup at `~/.claude/identities/tina/bounties/identity-modal-wakeup-form-editor/prototype.html` served over the tailnet — Ashley signed off "ship it" verbatim, so the prototype is the locked source of truth for form shape / field layout / per-type conditional swap / live JSON preview / silent Intl-based timezone detection. Two atomic commits: `1bfffc0` (backend widen — `WakeupUpdate` type + WS validator + wire payload extended to accept `name?` + `instruction?`) then `8b1989e` (frontend rewrite — WakeupsTab.tsx form editor + widened `OnUpdate` signature in IdentityModal.tsx + new WakeupsTab.test.tsx). Form fields (edit mode): editable `name` text input; schedule-type dropdown (interval/daily/weekly/one_shot) that swaps per-type fields underneath — interval: number + unit picker (s/m/h/d); daily: `<input type="time">`; weekly: day-of-week dropdown + time; one_shot: `<input type="datetime-local">` with inline red "fires this immediately" hint on past values — editable `instruction` textarea; secondary `enabled` checkbox (in addition to the always-visible header on/off chip which stays untouched from patch #154). Timezone: NO dropdown UI. Silently detected via `Intl.DateTimeFormat().resolvedOptions().timeZone` on mount, fallback `'America/New_York'` if Intl returns undefined/empty/'UTC'. Written into `schedule.timezone` on daily/weekly/one_shot saves; OMITTED from interval schedules (scheduler no-ops timezone on interval per tina.md § Scheduled wake-ups). Detected TZ shown as small muted hint under per-type fields on daily/weekly/one_shot only. Live JSON preview alongside the form (right column ≥620px, stacked below on mobile) — updates every keystroke via `useMemo`, matches the exact wire payload shape. Client-side shallow validation blocks Save on obvious errors (interval `every` regex, daily/weekly `at` regex, weekly `day` allowlist, one_shot `at` non-NaN parse) — server remains authoritative. `hydrateFormSchedule` round-trips existing schedules back into form fields; unrecognized shapes silently fall back to `{type: 'daily', at: '09:00'}` on entry — acceptable trade for a simple entry path since old hand-edited JSON is rare. Preserves patch #154's `useEffect` reset on server refresh (extended to also reset name + instruction drafts) and the "Scheduler reloads every ~30s" hint next to Save/Cancel. Backend widening was minimal: `identity-artifact-reader.ts` `WakeupUpdate` type + writer branch + remote-branch python one-liner already generic (`for k,v in u.items(): d[k]=v` merges any key); `claude-session-server.ts` WS handler validators added for the two new optional fields; `claude-session-api.ts` `IdentityUpdateWakeupPayload.updates` type widened. **Executor auto-fix (Rule 1 — Bug):** Test 4's `getByText` matcher for the past-datetime hint used regex `/fires this immediately/i` which failed to match because the hint has an inline `<b>immediately</b>` splitting the phrase across DOM children; fixed by asserting the two fragments separately (`/that datetime is in the past/i` for the text node + `"immediately"` for the `<b>` inner text). Same assertion, valid split. | 260731-1c8 | Add title + avatar editing to the IdentityModal Identity tab. Ashley bounty-driven (`identity-editing-modal` — pinned, verbatim "there are only like two things that you can customize about identity within the app which is the avatar and the title and so just having a place to adjust that felt like it would be a good addition and we could put it in the modal that comes up when you click on like the avatar when you're already in the session"). Fifth atomic entry in the pinned-bounties multi-session work-through-all-pinned-bounties arc (after #215 copy-button + #216 overlay-behind-badge + #217 attach-inside-textarea + #218 alphabetical-row-ordering). Scope locked to EXACTLY two editable fields: `title` (tagline) + `avatar` (image). `displayName` deliberately NOT exposed as editable — Ashley verbatim: "I was not thinking of editing display name um because it ties into like what that identity is called but the title and the avatar could be adjusted." `colorHue` exists in the backend but out of scope. Wires to existing `PUT /identities/:id` backend endpoint (`src/backend/database/routes/identities.ts:170` — multer FormData with JSON `data` field for `{title}` + optional `avatar` file, PNG/JPEG/WebP, 2MB cap, MD5 etag; auth-gated with JWT + userId scoping). Follows patch #154's write-path idiom (wakeup editor + bounty priority editor in the SAME modal — "first WRITE path on identity artifacts") — mutation callback threaded from modal, fresh-state applied on success. Deviates only in transport: HTTP FormData rather than WebSocket. **Fix (commit `80c0327`):** `src/ui/features/pretty-view/IdentityModal.tsx` — added imports (updateIdentity + applyIdentityChange), editor state hooks (titleDraft, committedTitle, avatarDraft, savingState, saveError, avatarPreviewUrl), reset effect (syncs draft state when identity prop changes or modal opens), cleanup effect (revokes createObjectURL on unmount / success / cancel), onAvatarPick handler (accepts File, generates optimistic preview via URL.createObjectURL), onSave handler (builds FormData with `data: JSON.stringify({title: titleDraft.trim()})` + optional avatar file, calls updateIdentity, on success calls applyIdentityChange from @/state/identities-store to broadcast the updated row to every useIdentities consumer — IdentityBadge / SessionRow / PrettyConversationRow / RelayInboundBubble all update in one call, no separate refetch needed), onCancel handler (resets drafts + revokes preview URL). Header `<img>` cache-busted via `?v=${avatarEtag}` — since `avatarUrl` is `/identities/${id}/avatar` (same string across avatar changes), the query param forces browser to re-fetch after a successful save. Editor block added to Identity tab with always-editable inputs + Save/Cancel footer (planner picked shape over Edit-toggle for lighter-weight interaction). Deliberately NO client-preflight of file size/mime — server is source of truth, and tests explicitly assert the server error string flows through inline (via saveError state, not modal close). On failure typed title + picked file survive so retry doesn't lose the user's work. **Executor auto-fix (Rule 1 — Bug):** Save button disabled predicate originally compared `titleDraft` against the `identity.title` prop; after a successful save the parent prop doesn't re-render immediately, so the button wouldn't re-disable. Fixed by introducing `committedTitle` state that tracks the last successfully saved title and updating on save success. Tests: new file `src/ui/features/pretty-view/IdentityModal.test.tsx` (5-case suite) — edit-title happy path, avatar-upload FormData shape assertion (verifies the File is in the FormData), oversized-file inline error (>2MB triggers server-side 400 → error text renders in tab without modal close), wrong-mime inline error (gif triggers server-side 400 → error text renders inline), cancel-discards (typed title + picked file both reset when Cancel clicked, preview URL revoked). Verification: `npx tsc --noEmit` EXIT 0; backend files touched = 0 (endpoint already exists, so `npm run build:backend` skipped per plan); targeted `npx vitest run src/ui/features/pretty-view/IdentityModal.test.tsx` = 5/5 passed; full `npx vitest run 2>&1 | tee /tmp/fullsuite.log` = 880 passed / 6 skipped / 0 failed across 78 files (up from #218 baseline of 875, +5 = new IdentityModal test suite — checks out); independent grep of `/tmp/fullsuite.log` per tina.md L508 preference (#209→#211 arc) — 0 real failure markers (all `FAIL|failed|✗|Unhandled` matches inside passing test descriptions like `upload_failed`, `failed attempts`, `fetch failed`, or expected backend log lines from passing error-path tests). Single atomic commit `80c0327` on `feat/tab-title-from-tmux`. Scope check: `git show --name-only 80c0327` shows exactly the 2 declared files (`IdentityModal.tsx` + `IdentityModal.test.tsx`), both under `src/ui/features/pretty-view/` — no backend, no identity-side (`~/.claude/identities/tina/` clean per L578 executor-scope-audit), no scope creep. NO push, NO docker build, NO deploy at commit time — orchestrator handles bare push after per Ashley 2026-07-30 refinement (downstream consumption via Stacy/ceo-skynet, near-zero-blast-radius; skynet-ec2 push→build→recreate motion still HELD per handoff rule + Standing Directive against reflex deploy-recommendation on code-complete through-lines). Ships as patch #219 in `~/.claude/identities/tina/skynet-patches.md`. Closes pinned bounty `~/.claude/identities/tina/bounties/identity-editing-modal/` (Tina archives after this row + patch entry + bounty flip — orchestrator bookkeeping split off from executor per h8f-vs-hn8 learned preference; pin left intact on archived record per only-user-pins symmetric rule). Deploy queue extends to #198→#219 (44 unpushed-to-container commits); still HELD until Ashley's shape-lock ship word. | 260730-wfy | Universal alphabetical row ordering in the conversation list — activeSet, pinned, per-host grouped buckets, and RDP sentinel bucket all sort rows by `row.label` with `localeCompare(..., { numeric: true, sensitivity: "base" })` (case-insensitive, locale-aware, numeric-natural). Host ORDER in Tier 3 unchanged (hostTree walk order). Ashley bounty-driven (`preserve-convo-list-order-especially-pins` — pinned, verbatim "keep ordering the same in convo list, especially in pins"). Widened mid-discussion to universal alphabetical verbatim: "what I was going to argue for is just always alphabetical, no matter where you're looking, like we're talking alphabetical under a certain host, if it's not pinned yet or alphabetical, if things are active or alphabetical, if they're pinned. So you can always have some stability and even finding things easier in a long list because you know what the alphabet is." Root cause the fix closes: `computeSnapshot`'s Tier 1 + Tier 2 iterated in TWO passes (openTabs first, fleetSyntheticRows second), so a row's position in a tier depended on WHICH SOURCE currently owned it — clicking a fleet-only row to make it an openTab teleported the row within its tier. Same latent bug shape in Tier 3's byHostId buckets. Universal alphabetical kills all three at once and gives Ashley alphabet-based muscle memory. **Fix (commits `332aed5` + `b508312`):** module-scoped `compareByLabel` helper in `src/ui/state/conversation-store.ts` (`(a, b) => a.label.localeCompare(b.label, undefined, { numeric: true, sensitivity: "base" })`) referenced by all four sort sites: (a) `activeSetRows.sort(...)` after both source loops, (b) `pinned.sort(...)` after both source loops, (c) per-host `rows.sort(...)` inside the `orderedHosts` walk AND the orphan-host fallback loop over `byHostId` (both sorted BEFORE `grouped.push`), (d) `rdpRows.sort(...)` after both the hostTree pass + orphan `hostsFlat` pass, BEFORE the sentinel `grouped.push({ hostId: "__rdp__", ... })`. Header comment at `conversation-store.ts:6-7` rewritten to replace the outdated "No new sort rule, no recency shuffle, no alphabetical override" bullet with a bullet documenting the new alphabetical rule (locale-aware, case-insensitive, numeric-natural, applies within-bucket at every tier; host ORDER unchanged). Test file `conversation-store.test.ts`: hit-by-hit audit of all 28 `activeSet\[|pinned\[|grouped\[` grep hits documented in SUMMARY.md — 2 hits updated (lines 816, 820 — Test 25 union-rendering multi-row assertion where labels `terminal:hostA` vs `scratch` reordered from source-order `[t1, scratch]` to alphabetical `[scratch, t1]`); 26 hits left unchanged (single-row buckets, hostId/hostName/length assertions, or same-label ties). ONE new regression test `"pinned tier is alphabetically sorted by row.label regardless of source"` asserting two openTabs `["z","m"]` in source-order + two fleet sessions `["a","n"]` in source-order → `snap.pinned.map(r => r.label)` deep-equals `["a","m","n","z"]`; would fail against pre-change store which emitted `["z","m","a","n"]`. Panel test `PrettyConversationsPanel.test.tsx` audited but NOT modified — panel tests mock `useConversations` return directly, so the store sort logic doesn't reach them; mocks pass through unchanged. Verification: `npx tsc --noEmit` EXIT 0; `npm run build:backend` EXIT 0 (per Tina L456 preference — frontend tsc alone doesn't catch backend TS errors); `npm run build` EXIT 0; targeted `npx vitest run` on `conversation-store.test.ts` + `PrettyConversationsPanel.test.tsx` = 101 passed; full `npx vitest run --reporter=verbose` = 875 passed / 6 skipped / 0 failed across 77 files (up from #217 baseline of 874, +1 = new pinned-alphabetical regression test — checks out); independent grep of `/tmp/fullsuite.log` per tina.md L508 preference (#209→#211 arc) — 0 real failure markers (all `FAIL|failed|✗` matches are inside passing test descriptions like `upload_failed`, `failed attempts`, `fetch failed`, or expected backend log lines from passing error-path tests). Two atomic commits on `feat/tab-title-from-tmux`: `332aed5` (implementation + header comment) + `b508312` (test updates + regression test). Scope check: `git diff --name-only HEAD~2 HEAD` shows exactly the 2 declared files, both under `src/ui/state/` — no backend, no identity-side, no scope creep. Post-commit: bare push to `origin/feat/tab-title-from-tmux` = `bd0dc04..b508312` (authorized per Ashley 2026-07-30 refinement — downstream consumption via Stacy/ceo-skynet, near-zero-blast-radius; skynet-ec2 push→build→recreate motion still HELD). Ships as patch #218 in `~/.claude/identities/tina/skynet-patches.md`. So it's similar to the send button, you know, instead of trying to like put an actual button on the left side." — so the design shifted from "sibling row-2 button" to "inside-textarea absolute button" matching Send's #129 wrapper-specificity workaround pattern (bare `<button>` not shadcn Button; `absolute left-1 bottom-0.5` mirroring Send's `right-1 bottom-0.5`; `p-2` for 40×40 hit target around a `size-6` 24×24 Paperclip icon matching Send's paper-plane, NOT the removed aux-group's `size-4`). Four edits shipped atomically in commit `575baff`: (1) removed the `{showPaperclip && <Button ...>}` block from Row 1's `flex flex-row gap-1` aux group (was ~L1345-1378) and updated the group's leading comment to reflect the move + preserve Patch #83/#84 markers; (2) added a new bare `<button type="button">` inside the Row 2 `<div className="relative flex-1 self-stretch">` wrapper as a sibling of the Send button, with `aria-label="Attach file"` / `title="Attach file"` / `onClick={handleOpenFilePicker}` / `disabled={canSend===false || asideActive===true || recycleActive===true}` all preserved VERBATIM so existing paperclip tests (Tests 3/4/5 in `ComposeBox.test.tsx` checking `getByLabelText(/attach file/i)`) keep passing unchanged, plus subtle color `text-[rgba(240,235,224,0.3)] hover:text-[rgba(240,235,224,0.9)]` mirroring Send's non-morphed branch and matching disable/transition classes; (3) added conditional `showPaperclip && "pl-10"` to the Textarea className adjacent to the existing `"pr-10"` (patch #129) so the textarea's LEFT reserves 40px hit-target space for the new Paperclip only when it renders — desktop-without-paperclip loses no usable width; (4) added Test 4b regression guard in `ComposeBox.test.tsx` between Test 4 and Test 5 asserting Paperclip's closest `.relative.flex-1` ancestor also contains the Send button — fails if a future refactor silently moves Paperclip back to Row 1 or into a separate wrapper. | 260730-ujq | Copy-to-clipboard button on code blocks and blockquotes inside pretty-view chat messages. Ashley bounty-driven (`code-blocks-copy-button` — parked earlier session via `/bounty`, greenlit as first of the pinned-slate work-through-all-pinned-bounties arc she named this session). New file `src/ui/features/pretty-view/CopyableBlock.tsx` (~122 lines) — small React wrapper mounting a hover-visible copy button as an overlay on `<pre>` / `<blockquote>` renderers; `navigator.clipboard.writeText` on click with `window.electronClipboard` fallback (matches the three existing callsites at `HostKeyVerificationDialog.tsx:42`, `WarpgateDialog.tsx:34`, `ConnectionLog.tsx:69` — deliberately did NOT reach for `RobustClipboardProvider` which is xterm-specific and has focus-listener side effects); "Copy" ↔ "Copied" (with checkmark) label swap that reverts after 1500ms via a single `setTimeout` with a cleanup ref so unmount-during-copied doesn't leak. Styling via inline Tailwind arbitrary-value classes drawing from the pv-* palette (`bg-white/[0.06]`, `text-[color:var(--color-pv-fg)]`, `border-[color:var(--color-pv-border-quiet)]`) — deliberately did NOT touch `src/ui/index.css` (task-shape's mention was permissive; the tokens are consumed inline in the pretty-view Tailwind convention). Hover/touch discriminator uses pure CSS media queries (`@media (hover: hover)` for hover-visibility + `[@media(pointer:coarse)]:opacity-100` for always-visible on touch) instead of the `useIsTouchDevice` hook — plumbing a hook through a `ReactMarkdown` `components` override would require prop-drilling or context, CSS is simpler and matches the "SOLE mobile-vs-desktop discriminator" note at `ComposeBox.tsx:193`. Wiring in `src/ui/features/pretty-view/ChatMessage.tsx` extends the existing `components={{ ... }}` override map on the `<ReactMarkdown>` invocation (already the extension pattern in use at lines 168-179 for `a`, `p`, etc.) with two new slots: `pre: CopyableBlock` and `blockquote: CopyableBlock`. Import added at top. `<ReactMarkdown>` was already loaded with `remark-gfm` — no new deps, no new imports beyond the local `CopyableBlock`. **Executor auto-fixes (per SUMMARY.md decisions block):** (1) fixed `textContent` extraction to clone the wrapper and remove the copy button before reading — otherwise the "Copy"/"Copied" button label would bleed into the clipboard payload, defeating the feature. (2) used a `window.navigator` Proxy pattern in tests (not `Object.defineProperty`) because in vitest+jsdom, the component module sees the real jsdom `Clipboard [EventTarget]` through a different reference chain than the test module, so patching `navigator.clipboard` directly had no effect on what the component called. Tests: 8 new behavior tests (A-F) in `CopyableBlock.test.tsx` covering render, click-copy on both clipboard paths, 1500ms Copied-state revert, textContent extraction correctness, swallowed-rejection safety on clipboard error. 3 new regression-guard tests (G-I) in `ChatMessage.test.tsx` inside a new describe block confirming: `<pre>` renders wrapped with copy button, `<blockquote>` renders wrapped with copy button, injected-turn / quick-reply / ThumbsUp short-circuits remain untouched. Verification: `npx tsc --noEmit` EXIT 0; CopyableBlock.test.tsx 8/8; ChatMessage.test.tsx 11/11 (8 pre-existing + 3 new); full `npx vitest run` = 872 passed / 6 skipped / 0 failed (up from prior #214 baseline of 861 — +8 CopyableBlock + +3 ChatMessage = +11, checks out); log grep-gate `FAIL\|failed\|✗\|Unhandled Rejection` returned zero real failures per the just-promoted L508 learned preference (don't trust bare "0 failed" summary lines, especially on patches touching new async surface area). Two atomic commits on `feat/tab-title-from-tmux`: `0212822` (Task 1 — CopyableBlock component + tests) + `26cd804` (Task 2 — ChatMessage wiring + regression tests). Scope check: `git diff --name-only HEAD~2 HEAD` shows exactly the 4 declared files, all under `src/ui/features/pretty-view/` — no `src/backend/` touch, no `~/.claude/identities/tina/**` touch, no scope creep into `src/ui/index.css`. NO push, NO docker build, NO deploy — stopped at commit boundary per fleet rule (Ashley 2026-07-27). Ships as patch #215 in `~/.claude/identities/tina/skynet-patches.md`. | 260730-sjf | Fixed the session-recycling overlay firing spuriously on host-reachability trouble — two categorical fixes shipped as one arc. Ashley bounty-driven (`recycling-overlay-fires-when-host-unreachable` — parked earlier this session via `/bounty`, greenlit after diagnosis). Root cause: Layer-2 discovery repoll's `.then()` callback in `src/backend/claude-session/claude-session-server.ts` conflated "we couldn't reach the host to ask" with "we asked and Claude isn't there" — both funneled into `transitionToHolding("discovery_diff")` and armed the SessionHoldingOverlay after its 350ms delay-arm. Once armed on a false signal, the next tick returned `active`+SAME sessionFile → fell into the "same file, still active — no state change here" branch that deliberately did NOT clear holding → overlay stuck for the full 5-min holdingTimeout (or until user hit Reset). Two categorical feeders of the false-arm: (i) `exec_error` from the three SSH round-trip try/catches inside `discoverClaudeSession` (walk / PID-file / JSONL test), (ii) `no_tmux_session` from `queryPanePid` returning null on ANY SSH throw at `src/backend/ssh/tmux-helper.ts:219`. **Fix A (commit `4d3ef07`) — narrow the arm at the backend.** `queryPanePid` split its single catch into a two-case contract: `execCommand` throw → RETHROW so the caller can classify; unparseable/empty pane_pid → return null (legitimate "no such pane"). JSDoc updated with the two-case contract. `queryNewestTmuxSession`'s catch left unchanged (backward-compat with its own callers). `discoverClaudeSession` wraps the `queryPanePid` call in try/catch — SSH throw → `{status:"inactive", reason:"exec_error"}` (same category as the three existing SSH-throw catches); genuine null → existing `no_tmux_session` return unchanged. `ClaudeSessionDiscoveryResult` JSDoc block updated to note `exec_error` is the categorical "we couldn't reliably ask" signal funneling all four SSH-throw sites. `claude-session-server.ts` repoll callback hoists `const isExecErrorTick = result.status === "inactive" && result.reason === "exec_error"` at top of `.then` body — inactive branch now switches on reason: `exec_error` = silent tick (NO transitionToHolding, early return); all other reasons (`not_claude`, `no_pid_session_file`, `no_open_session_file`, `no_tmux_session`) keep existing `transitionToHolding("discovery_diff")` behavior. `holdingTicks++` block guarded on `!isExecErrorTick` so SSH failures don't burn the 5-minute holding budget on ticks with no diagnostic signal. Two tests added to `session-file-discovery.test.ts` (Cases 13+14). **Fix B (commit `90e1d85`) — self-clear on same-file recovery.** New WS event variant `{type:"session_holding_cleared"}` added as `SessionHoldingClearedEvent` type + wired into `ClaudeSessionServerEvent` union in `src/ui/api/claude-session-api.ts`. New backend helper `transitionFromHoldingToActiveSameFile()` modeled on `transitionToHolding` (matching idempotency guard `if (changeoverState !== "holding") return;`) — flips `changeoverState="active"`, resets `holdingTicks=0`, emits the new WS frame (same try/catch shape as transitionToHolding), logs `claude_session_holding_cleared` at info level. Call site: repoll active-branch's same-file fall-through path now `else if (changeoverState === "holding")` on the same-file compare invokes the new helper. Real SIGTERM-fallback recycles unaffected because they take the different-file branch first. Frontend: new `case "session_holding_cleared"` in PrettyView's `ws.onmessage` switch calls `setIsHolding(false)` + `setHoldingTimeoutError(false)` ONLY — deliberately does NOT wipe messages / contextPct / harnessTasks / backgroundedAgents / plan_pending / asideText / setStatus (the point is the session did NOT actually change; message stream survives verbatim). Distinct from case `session_changed` which is heavy-reset for a real recycle. **Auto-fixes (per SUMMARY.md decisions block):** (1) rather than mock full WS server + SSH pair (7+ dependency mocks to reach `connectToPane`), the executor exported a module-scope `__applyRepollResultForTests` pure test seam that takes state + helpers as injectable parameters, mirroring the existing `__handleIdentityCountBountiesForTests` pattern — the codebase's established test-seam idiom. All 5 spec-required branch cases (a-e) covered plus 4 additional sub-cases in the new `claude-session-server.repoll.test.ts` (9 tests total). (2) PrettyView F3 contextPct-preservation test initially asserted `container.textContent.toContain('42')` and failed because contextPct renders via `aria-valuenow={contextPct}` on the context bar, not as visible text; fixed to `container.querySelector('[aria-valuenow="42"]')`. 3 new PrettyView tests total (F1: overlay unmounts on session_holding_cleared while isHolding=true; F2: messages preserved verbatim; F3: contextPct preserved via aria-valuenow). Verification: `npm run build:backend` EXIT 0, zero errors (critical because `tsc --noEmit` alone doesn't catch backend TS errors per patch #154 rule); `npm run build` EXIT 0, 4.39s; full `npm test` = 76 files / 861 passed / 6 skipped / 0 failed; log grep-gate `FAIL|failed|✗` returned 0 real failures (verified per the just-promoted L508 learned preference from #211 arc — don't trust summary lines without a spot-check). Note: the 5 unrelated ComposeBox.voice.test.tsx failures noted in the prior task's row (from patch #209's jsdom .catch bug) are already gone at this branch's tip, resolved by patch #211's `9213a5b`. Two atomic commits on `feat/tab-title-from-tmux`: `4d3ef07` (Fix A backend narrowing) + `90e1d85` (Fix B self-clear). Scope check: `git diff --name-only 9213a5b..HEAD` shows exactly the 8 declared files (tmux-helper.ts, session-file-discovery.ts, claude-session-server.ts, claude-session-api.ts, PrettyView.tsx, session-file-discovery.test.ts, claude-session-server.repoll.test.ts, PrettyView.test.tsx) — no scope creep, no /opt/ touch, no `~/.claude/identities/` touch. Mirrors patch #137's session-working-store pattern byte-for-byte: new module-scoped `src/ui/state/session-recycling-store.ts` with `publishSessionRecycling(key, isRecycling)`, `useSessionRecycling(key)` (useSyncExternalStore hook with null-key short-circuit), `getSessionRecyclingSnapshot()` test-only accessor, `__resetForTest()`. Consumer: `PrettyConversationsPanel.tsx` `PrettyConversationRowLive` reads `useSessionRecycling(sessionKey)` alongside existing `useSessionWorking(sessionKey)`; single key `${host.id}:${targetTmuxSession ?? ""}` resolves both stores. Coerces null→false at forward with `isRecycling === true` since row prop is strict boolean-optional. | 260730-ptd | Wired 4 Google Material Design audio-feedback sounds (start/stop/cancel/error) into the compose-box voice-recording flow at `src/ui/features/pretty-view/useVoiceRecording.ts`. Ashley bounty-driven ("sounds for mic functionality" — parked 2026-07-29 via `/bounty`, picked up this session). Sourced from Google's Material Design sound library (CC-BY-SA 4.0, unmodified from https://archive.org/details/material-design-sound-resources) after Ashley rejected two prior audition rounds (Mixkit legacy notification tones + uisfx modern packs). Chosen for the "big-tech app that actually gets it" aesthetic she was pointing at — short percussive taps, no melodic content, purpose-built for start-of-action feedback. Meta's Sound Kit was denied access on their Drive folder so not viable. Pairing: `state-change_confirm-down` → start (feels like purposeful press to arm recording); `state-change_confirm-up` → stop (Material's designed sonic pair to confirm-down, resolves the down-press cleanly); `navigation-cancel` → cancel (literally named for the purpose); `alert_error-02` → error (middle of the 3 error variants, shortest file, likely subtlest — easy swap to -01 or -03 if it feels wrong later). Task 1 (`be3c573`): copied 4 unmodified MP3s into new `src/ui/assets/sounds/mic/` dir + wrote `CREDITS.md` with attribution text covering source URL, license URL, unmodified-from-source note, and verbatim original→destination filename mapping. Source-tree attribution only for now; UI-visible credit surface (About page etc.) deferred to whenever About surface is next touched. Task 2 (`5c59743`): 4 Vite `?url` imports at hook top (built into Vite, no config change); 4 `useRef<HTMLAudioElement | null>(null)` slots with inline lazy-init guards (hook mount is the only construction site, refs survive re-renders without reconstruction — cheaper than useMemo and no re-render triggers); `playSound(audio)` helper centralizes `currentTime = 0` reset (enables replay without seeking on rapid-fire transitions) + `.play().catch(() => {})` silent-failure (audio failure is UX polish, MUST NOT disrupt recording — Safari can reject if user hasn't yet interacted with the page, and a failed play() throwing would tank a working recording). Placements per plan spec (referenced by function name in `useVoiceRecording.ts`): start.mp3 in the `start()` `.then()` callback AFTER `recorder.start()` + `setState("recording")` AND ONLY on success path — permission-denied path (getUserMedia rejection) NEVER plays start.mp3 because it never enters the `.then` (per plan constraint: "so we don't play on permission-denied paths"; also explicitly regression-tested); stop.mp3 as the very FIRST statement in `endAppend()` AND `endSend()` after their state guards (BEFORE any `await`, giving user immediate feedback before the STT network round-trip latency); cancel.mp3 as the first statement in `cancel()` after its guard (BEFORE `stopRecording()` teardown); error.mp3 in all 3 failure branches of `transcribeBlob()` (fetch throw, !res.ok, invalid JSON) + the empty-transcript guard in endAppend/endSend. iOS silent-switch handling: ZERO code added — HTML5 `<audio>` on iOS Safari uses the ambient audio-session category which honors the physical silent switch by default; adding explicit AudioSession config would only be needed if we wanted sounds to bypass the switch (which we don't, per plan spec). Explicitly out of scope (deferred): mute toggle, transcript-ready chime (5th sound), UI-visible attribution surface. Test suite `useVoiceRecording.test.ts` grew 8→14 tests: `Audio` constructor mocked using a function-declaration `class`-shape (NOT `vi.fn().mockImplementation(() => ({}))` — vitest arrow mocks are not valid constructors, `new AudioMock()` on an arrow throws; this scarred the executor mid-test-write and is captured as a Rule-3-adjacent implementation decision in SUMMARY.md's decisions block); each new test uses `mock.invocationCallOrder` to prove ordering guarantees (start.mp3 AFTER recorder.start(); stop.mp3 BEFORE fetch call in both endAppend AND endSend; cancel.mp3 BEFORE recorder.stop()). Regression tests: getUserMedia rejection path never invokes start.mp3.play(); HTTP 500 response invokes error.mp3.play(); network error invokes error.mp3.play(); rejected-play() promise is silently swallowed AND state still transitions to "recording" (proving audio failure doesn't corrupt state). Verification: full frontend `npx vitest run` = 74 files / 841 passed (+6 vs baseline of 835) + 6 skipped / 0 failed; `npx tsc --noEmit` EXIT 0; hook grep gates from plan's done-block all pass. Backend build NOT run (patch touches zero backend files — per patch #154 rule, frontend-only patches validated with `tsc --noEmit` + vitest). Scope check: `git diff --name-only HEAD~2 HEAD` shows only 7 declared files (4 MP3s + CREDITS.md + hook + test) — no `src/backend/` touched, no `~/.claude/identities/tina/**` touched by the executor. | 260730-mzj | Replaced the mtime-based JSONL discovery step in `src/backend/claude-session/session-file-discovery.ts` (`discoverClaudeSession` Step 5) with a per-PID session-file lookup, so pretty-view stops mis-serving one agent's transcript when another agent shares its cwd on the same box. Ashley reported clicking Aqua on Workstation → seeing Wilma's session bubbles; queried `/sessions/list` from tina's admin cookie and confirmed Workstation has 20 tmux sessions incl. both aqua and wilma, both launching claude from the same home cwd → JSONLs colliding in the shared `~/.claude/projects/-home-<user>/` slug directory → old `ls -t | head -n 1` picking whichever wrote most recently (Wilma). This is a recurrence of the archived bounty `pretty-view-shows-wrong-session-jsonl` (dropped 2026-07-26 when Nelly's trigger — an unattended backup-review timer sharing her cwd — was retired; its own timeline note predicted "re-open only if another unattended-claude-in-shared-cwd pattern reappears," and it did). Fix leverages Claude Code v2.1.150+'s per-PID note at `~/.claude/sessions/<PID>.json` — `{"pid":..., "sessionId":<UUID>, "cwd":<abs-path>, ...}` — where `sessionId` IS the JSONL filename. New Step 5 flow: `cat ~/.claude/sessions/<PID>.json` (with `---HOME---\n$HOME` delimiter appended in the same exec to avoid a second roundtrip), parse JSON, extract `sessionId` + `cwd`, slugify `cwd` via `replace(/[./]/g, "-")` (byte-identical semantics to old `sed 's|[./]|-|g'`), construct `$HOME/.claude/projects/<slug>/<sessionId>.jsonl`, verify existence with `test -f` in a second exec. Steps 1-4 (`queryPanePid` → BFS descendant-tree walk → parse `claude` PID) preserved byte-identical, including the load-bearing awk `;`-separator comment that scarred patch #170→#174 (fleet-wide outage from JS `+`-concat of awk statements without terminators). New Step 5 script block carries a matching JS-concat discipline comment that cross-references the walkScript's comment so a future maintainer doing a "cleanup" pass understands both blocks share the same assembly hazard. `ClaudeSessionDiscoveryResult` inactive `reason` union widened with `"no_pid_session_file"` (missing / unreadable / malformed / no `sessionId` field on the PID note); existing `"no_open_session_file"` reused for the narrower case where `sessionId` resolved but the `.jsonl` file doesn't exist on disk. Consumer at `claude-session-server.ts` uses generic `result.status` / `result.reason` branching (no exhaustive switch), so no consumer changes were required — the widened union stays a discriminated-union-safe extension. Old `ls -t $HOME/.claude/projects/<slug>/*.jsonl | head -n 1` dropped entirely — Ashley confirmed 2026-07-30 that no fleet box runs pre-2.1.150 Claude, so no backcompat fallback needed (single code path). Discovery-repoll ticker at `claude-session-server.ts:2201-2267` explicitly NOT retired — even with correct-first-time PID-file lookup, the ticker still serves the "pane's claude PID changed after session recycle, re-discover" case; out of scope for this task. Task 2: full rewrite of `src/backend/claude-session/session-file-discovery.test.ts` — 12 tests total (6 preserved walk-step cases + 6 new Step 5 branches). New tests: Test 7 = happy path with explicit slug-transform assertion (verifies `/home/foo` + `. ` in path both slugify correctly and land at the right JSONL path); Test 8 = PID-file missing / cat returned no delimiter → `no_pid_session_file`; Test 9 = PID-file malformed JSON → `no_pid_session_file`; Test 10 = PID-file present but missing `sessionId` field → `no_pid_session_file`; Test 11 = `sessionId` resolved but `test -f` says the JSONL is absent → `no_open_session_file`; Test 12 = PID-file exec rejects (SSH error) → `exec_error`. `mockExecCommand` now dispatches on `".claude/sessions/"` and `'if [ -f "'` substrings; the old `"readlink -f"` / `"ls -t"` / `"/proc/"` dispatch keys deleted along with the source code they exercised. Verification: `npm run build:backend` EXIT 0, `npm run build` EXIT 0 (backend + frontend both — strict per patch #154 rule; frontend `tsc --noEmit` alone doesn't catch backend TS errors), `npx vitest run src/backend/claude-session/session-file-discovery.test.ts` 12/12 pass, sibling suites (`claude-session-server.count-bounties.test.ts`, `claude-session-server.aside.test.ts`, `claude-session-server.aside.integration.test.ts`, `identity-artifact-reader.test.ts`) 41/41 pass — no consumer regressions. Two new lucide-react buttons (Target = `/bounty`, ListPlus = `/queue`) inserted between the existing Lightbulb "explain" quick-reply and the Hourglass queue-until-idle button in `src/ui/features/pretty-view/ComposeBox.tsx` (import extended L2 with `Target, ListPlus` alphabetized into the named import; button pair added L~1471-1541 between Lightbulb's closing `</Button>` and the leading `{/* Patch #84: Queue button …` comment; order is Target FIRST then ListPlus per spec). Both buttons mirror the Lightbulb button's structure verbatim: `size="icon-sm"`, `variant="outline"`, identical `className` block (warm-neutral Glass gradient + hover variants + `max-md:size-12 [&_svg]:max-md:size-6` mobile bump). `onClick` handlers wire straight into the existing `handleSend(overridePayload?: string)` seam at `ComposeBox.tsx:795` — Target calls `handleSend("/bounty " + text)` and ListPlus calls `handleSend("/queue " + text)` with a single space between prefix and text. Using the overridePayload seam means D-50 newline collapse (Ink safety), COMPOSE-04 hard-lock (no optimistic bubble on send), attachment branching, and textarea-clear-on-success all apply automatically — no new state, no new function, no duplication of send-path plumbing. Disable predicate: `text.trim() === "" || canSend === false || asideActive === true || recycleActive === true` — spec only required the first two clauses (need content to prepend + match aside-morph disable pattern), but planner widened it to include the last two clauses to match every other WS-side-effecting aux-row button (ThumbsUp/Lightbulb/Hourglass all include `canSend` and `recycleActive`); planner's discretion, documented in PLAN's `<context>`. `aria-label` + `title`: `"Send with /bounty prefix"` for Target, `"Send with /queue prefix"` for ListPlus — mirrors the existing `Send with /id reset prefix` convention at line 1203, clustering the new buttons semantically with the Reset cell as the "prefix modifier" family of affordances (distinct from ThumbsUp/Lightbulb which send canned text ignoring textarea contents). Leading block comment above Target explains prefix-send affordance category. Leading block comment above ListPlus acknowledges naming collision — the existing Hourglass button (patch #84) is ALSO internally called "queue" but is a LOCAL "queue-until-idle" mechanic (single-slot pending message that fires when target session goes idle for 3s), semantically completely different from the new `/queue` slash-command which is a fleet-wide agent-side skill that files a task in the target agent's harness task list. Rename of Hourglass is explicitly out of scope; disambiguation via distinct icons + tooltips + inline naming-collision comment. | 260730-jes | Made deactivating an active conversation feel instant. Root cause: `doCloseTab(id)` in `src/ui/AppShell.tsx:1144` batched four state mutations (`setActiveTabId` + `selectConversation` + `setPaneTabIds` + `setTabs`) into a single React 18 commit alongside the Zustand `removeFromActiveSet(row.id)` that `handleRowDeactivate` at `PrettyConversationsPanel.tsx:453` had already fired. React rendered the whole batch as one commit — unmounting the deactivated PrettyView AND mounting the newly-activated conversation's PrettyView (fresh WS setup, backfill dispatch, hundreds of message bubbles, ResizeObserver spin-up, aside probe). The list paint was blocked behind all that mount work → ~1s frozen UI on Deactivate tap. Backend `deleteOpenTab(instanceId)` was already fire-and-forget async — NOT the bottleneck. Fix: added `startTransition` to the React named import (first `startTransition` use in the codebase — verified via `grep -rn "startTransition\|useTransition" src/` returned zero pre-change) at `AppShell.tsx:7`. Reshaped `doCloseTab` so the synchronous prelude (`tabs.find(...)`, `deleteOpenTab(...).catch(() => {})`, `terminalRefs.current.delete(id)` — read-only or Map-mutation, no React state) runs first, then a single `startTransition(() => { ... })` callback wraps all four state mutations plus the `remaining` / `nextId` computation that reads render-time closure. React now commits the urgent Zustand active-set removal (driving the list paint) as an urgent update FIRST, then commits the tab switch (triggering the expensive pane unmount+mount) as a transition. List settles + paints immediately; new pane fills in async. Accepted trade-off: for a fraction of a second between "list settled" and "new pane mounted," the right pane briefly shows the just-deactivated pretty view — acknowledged by Ashley ("the user isn't actually waiting on the session to be sort of unloaded"). 6-line WHY/WHAT/TRADE-OFF block comment above the `startTransition(...)` call names the intent + the "DO NOT REVERT to synchronous" warning + references bounty #5 so a future reader recognizes what's load-bearing. Task 2 test: Test 20H appended at the tail of the existing `describe("PrettyConversationsPanel: deactivate action (quick-260727-gm3)", ...)` block in `src/ui/features/pretty-conversations/PrettyConversationsPanel.test.tsx`. Uses fleet-derived fixture row (host id `"7"` numeric-parseable to match `fleetRowId(parseInt(row.host.id, 10), ...)` shape after a planner-caught NaN fix from initial `"h1"` fixture; matches Test 20F's pattern; tmuxSession `"ordering-session"`) exercising BOTH `removeFromActiveSet` calls (row.id purge AND fleet::7::ordering-session purge). Asserts via `vi.spyOn` + `mock.invocationCallOrder`: `removeFromActiveSetSpy` called twice with `toHaveBeenNthCalledWith(1, "active-h")` and `(2, "fleet::7::ordering-session")`, `onDeactivateRow` called with the full row object, and the ordering contract `removeFromActiveSetSpy.mock.invocationCallOrder[0] < onDeactivateRow.mock.invocationCallOrder[0]` — the CALLER-side ordering guarantee that keeps the two-commit React split working end-to-end. This test defends against a future refactor swapping the order in `handleRowDeactivate` and collapsing the two commits back into one. Test path chosen (extend existing 1866-line PrettyConversationsPanel.test.tsx) over creating a new AppShell.test.tsx from scratch, which would have required ~50 lines of new scaffolding for AppShell's ~30 imports — disproportionate to the ordering assertion size. Two atomic commits on `feat/tab-title-from-tmux`: `8ceffca` (feat: impl — AppShell.tsx +42/-27 across L7 import + L~1153-1194 body reshape, net +15) and `7ed9fe5` (test: Test 20H — PrettyConversationsPanel.test.tsx +55/-0 across L~957-1011). | 260729-ig7 | Bar-anchored Skynet's pretty-view contextPct scraper regex to close the weekly-limit false-positive that mirrors nelly's vms-apps context-watch pre-fix bug (f88c928, relayed 2026-07-25). Root cause: `src/backend/claude-session/claude-session-server.ts` L2660-2703 scraped Claude Code's tmux status-line context% every ~3s via `tmux capture-pane`, taking the rightmost `(\d{1,3})%` on lines containing `context)`. When Claude Code appends a weekly-limit warning to that same line (e.g. `Opus 4.7 (1M context) │ skynet █████░ 17% ┃ youve used 95% of your weekly limit`), rightmost-wins picks 95 (the warning) instead of 17 (the meter). Rare in practice (weekly warnings uncommon) but the meter is a load-bearing UX signal — a lie in it costs trust in the whole indicator. Fix: extracted the parser to a pure helper `parseContextPct(paneText: string): number | null` in new file `src/backend/claude-session/context-pct-parser.ts` (64 lines, mirrors the `session-file-parser.ts` extract-to-helper pattern in the same directory), with bar-anchored regex `[█▉▊▋▌▍▎▏░]\s*(\d{1,3})%` — the % must be immediately preceded (with optional whitespace) by a Claude Code meter glyph. Live Claude Code only renders `█` and `░` today; the wider partial-fill glyph set matches nelly's regex and is defensive against future variants. Fallback loop's `if (!/[░█]/.test(line)) continue;` line filter dropped as redundant (bar-anchored regex can't match a line without any bar glyph). Range guard (null for <0 / >100 / non-finite) folded into the helper. Semantics preserved: 8-line tail (patch #56), primary `context)`-label pass then bar-glyph fallback, rightmost-per-line + last-matching-line wins across the 8-line window (patch #59's GSD milestone-bar collision fix still discriminates — both meters bar-anchored, rightmost still wins). `claude-session-server.ts` setInterval callback (L2660-2703) now delegates to the helper — 4-condition null/finite/range chain collapsed to `if (pct === null) return;`; interval (3s), `contextPctInFlight` guard, `ws.readyState !== WebSocket.OPEN` guard, `connSnapshot` capture, `execCommand` shape, `ws.send({type:"context_pct", pct})` emit, silent `.catch()`, `.finally()` all unchanged. Wire shape unchanged — PrettyView + ComposeBox untouched. Tests: new `context-pct-parser.test.ts` (89 lines, 10 vitest cases) — (1) primary path single-line bar-anchored → returns meter %, (2) **bug repro** meter + weekly-warning appended → 29 not 95, (3) patch #59 collision case → rightmost bar-anchored wins, (4) `context)` line with warning-only text no bar-adjacent % → null (no fabricated reading), (5) no `context)` no bar glyph unrelated % → null, (6) 100% upper edge → 100, (7) primary/fallback split (bar-anchored NN% but no `context)`) → matched by fallback, (8) 8-line tail (10-line pane, line 1 distractor + line 10 real) → returns line 10, (9) last-matching-line wins semantic, (10) range guard 250% → null. | 260729-hn8 | Fixed the pretty-view screenshot-upload path collision when multiple files with the same original name land in a single batch (bounty `pretty-view-upload-path-collision`, reported 2026-07-24 after Ashley pasted 2 Chrome-clipboard `image.png` files in one paste and the receiving agent saw both message attachments pointing at the SAME finalPath `112220-image.png`; second file silently overwrote the first). Root cause: `src/backend/ssh/pretty-view-upload.ts` `resolveNonCollidingFinal` at L290-302 only stat-checks disk to decide whether to add `-2/-3/…-10` suffixes, and inside `handleUploadStart`'s per-file loop at L465-479, each file `sftpStat`s the SAME candidate path — File 2 sees "not exists" because File 1 has only claimed a `tempPath` (which is uniquely disambiguated by `randSuffix` at L483) but has not yet been renamed to its `finalPath` (that happens at completion via `sftpRename` at L671). Both files then rename to the same final target at completion; second write wins. Fix (batch-local reservation): threaded a `reservedFinalPaths: Set<string>` third argument through `resolveNonCollidingFinal`; every candidate check (primary + `n=2..10` suffix loop) now gates on BOTH `!sftpStat.exists` AND `!reserved.has(candidate)`; declared `const reservedFinalPaths = new Set<string>()` before the per-file loop and `reservedFinalPaths.add(finalPath)` immediately after each successful resolve. Reuses the existing `-2/-3/…` suffix strategy — behaviorally consistent with the on-disk collision path that Test 8 already documents (`143211-log.txt` → `143211-log-2.txt`). Tests added: Test 8b — two `image.png` files in one batch land at `143211-image.png` and `143211-image-2.png` (distinct, order-independent), both temp files write successfully, both rename to distinct final paths at completion, message frame shows two distinct `landingPath` values. Test 8c — three-way collision → `X`, `X-2`, `X-3` (covers the suffix loop iteration). Existing Test 8 disk-collision case still passes — fix is additive to the existing suffix logic, not a replacement. Verification: `npm run build:backend` clean, `npm run build` clean (patch #154 rule: backend files touched → run BOTH), `npx vitest run src/backend/ssh/pretty-view-upload.test.ts` = 15/15 pass. | 260729-1vd | Weekly usage meter — dual-race split-bar (5h + Week) inside `.pv-panel-header` below title row. Design iterated live with Ashley via `?metertry=1` container-injected tuner across variants A-G and positions 1-4, locked on F+4. Each bar: upper half warm-coral usage fill (`linear-gradient(90deg, hsla(38,60%,72%,0.85), hsla(20,72%,66%,0.95))` + `0 0 10px hsla(20,70%,60%,0.45)` glow), lower half cool-cyan elapsed-time fill (`linear-gradient(90deg, hsla(200,50%,60%,0.75), hsla(190,60%,62%,0.85))` + `0 0 8px hsla(190,60%,55%,0.35)` glow), 1px `rgba(0,0,0,0.25)` hairline divider at 50% — "racing" visual so pace-vs-elapsed reads at a glance (usage bar leading time bar = burning fast; lagging = on track). Ashley's original ask verbatim: "we basically have two bars where one is for the five-hour limit and one is for the weekly limit because both are important... each bar is actually split in half long ways so that the top inner bar represents actually how much of the given limit you've used, and the bottom inner bar represents how far along you are in that period of time. So you can kind of see them racing a little bit." Backend adds `/api/usage` Express proxy to Nelly's collector at `http://100.113.23.63:9421/` (source-IP gated to tailnet, no auth; response shape `{five_hour:{used_percentage,resets_at}, seven_day:{...}, updated_at, source_box}`; one Anthropic account = one global bucket that the whole fleet drains from — hence the visible meter). 5s AbortController timeout, 503 `{error:"usage collector unreachable"}` fallback on failure. Nginx routing added in BOTH `docker/nginx.conf` AND `docker/nginx-https.conf` (`location = /api/usage` proxying to `:30001`) per CLAUDE.md hard constraint that missing either causes the 200-with-index.html-and-frontend-crash-on-.map trap. Frontend polls `/api/usage` every 15s via `useEffect` + `setInterval` + AbortController cleanup, computes `elapsedPct = (now_s - (resets_at - window_s)) / window_s * 100` clamped `[0,100]` (window_s = 18000 for 5h, 604800 for 7d — rolling windows, not calendar-anchored; Anthropic-side `resets_at` is authoritative). On fetch failure retains last-known values, renders aria-busy empty container on first-load failure (no crash, no infinite spinner). `.pv-panel-header` restructured from single-row `flex` to `flex-direction:column; align-items:stretch; gap:8px` with new inner `.pv-panel-header-row` wrapper holding the previous `.pv-title` + `.pv-header-actions` content — preserves patch #142 `data-sidebar-toggle-overlaps` conditional padding-left behavior. 4 atomic commits on `feat/tab-title-from-tmux`: `761b9e7` (backend proxy route + BOTH nginx configs), `fe6da43` (CSS — header restructure + `.pv-usage-meter*` styles verbatim from tuner spec), `9b55766` (`WeeklyUsageMeter.tsx` component + panel wire-up), `91d6eb3` (4 new tests). Test suite green: `npx vitest run src/ui/features/pretty-conversations/` = 4 files / **67 passed** (36 pre-existing panel tests unchanged + 4 new WeeklyUsageMeter tests covering render / elapsed% math (80% ±1% given known now+resets_at) / fetch-failure C1 (first-load → aria-busy empty state) / fetch-failure C2 (subsequent → last-known retained)). Pre-push typecheck per patch #154 fixup lesson: `npm run build:backend` EXIT 0 + `npm run build` EXIT 0 (backend edits touched `src/backend/` so both build gates required). Design source of truth: `~/.claude/identities/tina/bounties/weekly-usage-meter/metertry-inject.html` `variantDualRace` function — CSS values pulled 1:1. Data source confirmed via Matrix relay DM with Nelly (owner of the collector on thenasty), 4-question audit answered end-to-end (endpoint / semantics / scope / stand-up-vs-consume) before any code touched. Reference bounty and thread: `~/.claude/identities/tina/bounties/weekly-usage-meter/`. NO push, NO docker build, NO deploy — stopped at commit boundary per fleet rule (Ashley 2026-07-27, "deploy pre-work is not authorized by a code-work ask"). Bundles with sibling commit `9f1fdd4` (selection-indicator brighter D variant — 3px hue ring + 72px glow on `.pv-row.selected`, iterated in the same session via `?seltune=1` tuner) for a single `feat/tab-title-from-tmux` push + full canonicalizing docker build + `docker compose up -d --force-recreate skynet` on Ashley's ship signal. Both patches ship as consecutive numbered entries in `~/.claude/identities/tina/skynet-patches.md` inline at deploy time (fleet rule: docs inline with ship, never batched). | 260728-0uo | Surgical mobile `.prose-sm` font bump for Telegram parity. Ashley's ask verbatim: "make the font bigger across the board on mobile, because Telegram's is bigger, and I like the size of that, if you can find out what it is." Five prototype revs (v1→v5, all served on tailnet 8899 from `~/.claude/identities/tina/bounties/skynet-transformation/`) drove the design: v1/v2/v3 ruled out font swap (Inter Variable vs system-ui/SF Pro looked identical to Ashley at every candidate size); v4 gave measurements (Skynet coded 14px `text-sm`, Telegram = iOS body 17px via `font: -apple-system-body`, ratio 1.214× at prototype viewport 440×796); v5 loaded Skynet's actual compiled CSS bundle (docker-cp'd out of running container at `/app/html/assets/index-3F5IQHC5.css`) so prototype bubble A was byte-identical to a deployed ChatMessage — Ashley still saw A as MUCH bigger than her real Skynet, ruling out class-list divergence. Final diagnostic: injected `?fontdiag=1` conditional script into container's `/app/html/index.html` (backed up as `index.html.pre-fontdiag.bak`) that measured her real bubble live and displayed on-page. Result: html `fs-lg dark`, html fs 16px, bubble fs 14px, p rect h 25px, viewport **587×1061** (1.334× BIGGER than my prototype's 440×796 on the same phone — explains why identical CSS px looked physically smaller in her Skynet render; still unexplained viewport delta, not the same as PWA-standalone-vs-Safari — she confirmed both look identical to her). Then evolved to `?fonttune=1` live tuner (peach panel with html font-size buttons 16/18/20/22/24/26/28/30/32 that mutated `document.documentElement.style.fontSize` in real-time on the running app) — she picked **html=24** as her Telegram-parity match (which cascades `.prose-sm` from 0.875rem to 21px).
